## 🌟 ¿Por qué postularse aquí?
Encontrar el trabajo adecuado es difícil. Hacemos el trabajo pesado recopilando las mejores oportunidades remotas de las principales empresas y startups de IA.

- **💰 Compensación del Top 1%:** Roles que comienzan en $40/hr y superan los $100/hr.
- **🌍 100% Remoto:** Trabaja desde cualquier lugar con tu propio horario.

> 🤝 **Nota de Transparencia:** Los enlaces proporcionados son de referidos. Si te postulas y te contratan, ¡podemos ganar una pequeña comisión sin ningún costo para ti! Usar estos enlaces ayuda a mantener este repositorio gratuito y actualizado diariamente. ¡Agradeceríamos mucho si los usas! ❤️

## IA y Machine Learning

### <a href="https://jobs.micro1.ai/post/0b30881e-512e-459e-be5d-108c4a455b7b?referralCode=7af9281b-9e44-4bed-80b8-dffcb900dc6f" target="_blank">AI Finance Domain Expert</a>
- **💸 Pago:** $100 - $200/hour
- **🔗 Postularse:** <a href="https://jobs.micro1.ai/post/0b30881e-512e-459e-be5d-108c4a455b7b?referralCode=7af9281b-9e44-4bed-80b8-dffcb900dc6f" target="_blank">Haz clic aquí para postularte</a>
- **📝 Descripción:** 
  > Required skills: critical thinking, analytical reasoning, attention to detail, 22+, $100-200/h.  critical thinking analytical reasoning attention to detail quality assurance written communication technica documentation prompt authoring & refinement AI Output Evaluation Professional Writing Report Writing Business Communication Problem Solving Content Review Data Interpretation Logical Resoning Professional Editing Independent Research Data Annotation Rubric-Based Evaluation Content Evaluation AI Model Evaluation Fact Checking RLHF Technical Editing Prompt Engineering About micro1 micro1 is the leading AI data lab for training frontier models and evaluating AI agents. Experts contribute their diverse subject matter knowledge across domains such as finance, healthcare, STEM engineering, and more. micro1 transforms that real-world expertise into high-quality training data, evaluations, and feedback loops that improve how AI systems learn, reason, and perform.  Our platform identifies and vets top talent through an AI recruiter, enabling high-quality expert contributions at scale. We aim to enable 1 billion people to do meaningful work by applying their expertise to AI. As our global expert network grows, micro1 is building the human intelligence layer for frontier AI.  Role Title: AI Finance Domain Expert  Role Type: Contractor (Part Time)  Location: Remote  micro1 is engaging AI Finance Domain Experts to contribute to a customer's project focused on enhancing AI-driven financial applications. In this role, you'll apply your expertise to help train next-generation AI systems. Your work will shape how models learn, reason, and perform through high-quality, real-world input. No prior experience in AI is required — your domain knowledge is what matters. This opportunity targets professionals with advanced financial acumen from private equity, venture capital, investment banking, equity research, corporate development, investment management, or strategic finance. Contributors will leverage their analytical rigor, financial judgment, and exceptional communication to ensure the quality and relevance of AI model outputs in finance.  Scope of Work  Analyze, review, and edit AI-generated financial content for accuracy, clarity, and relevance. Develop, refine, and evaluate AI prompts related to financial analysis, valuation, and investment decision-making. Assess and annotate complex financial data, reports, and model outputs using detailed rubrics and best practices. Author and review technical documentation, investment memos, due-diligence reports, and research summaries. Evaluate AI output for logical consistency, robustness, and adherence to professional financial standards. Conduct independent research and fact checking to validate information used by AI systems. Provide constructive feedback to optimize prompt engineering and improve model performance in finance use cases.  Preferred Qualifications  Minimum 3 years of experience in private equity, venture capital, investment banking, equity research, corporate development, investment management, or strategic finance. Track record of producing high-quality investment memos, valuation analyses, financial models, due-diligence reports, or market research. Demonstrated critical thinking, analytical reasoning, and attention to detail in fast-paced, data-driven environments. Exceptional written communication and professional editing skills, with proficiency in technical and business writing. Experience in prompt authoring, data annotation, content review, or evaluation of AI/model outputs is a strong advantage. Master’s, JD, MBA, PhD, or other advanced degree is a plus; candidates with backgrounds from highly selective universities or equivalent academic programs are encouraged to participate. Strong problem-solving ability, independent research experience, and a commitment to quality assurance and fact checking. Interested? Role Title: AI Data Science Domain Expert  Role Type: Contractor (Part Time)  Location: Remote  micro1 is engaging AI Data Science Domain Experts to participate in a customer's project focused on advancing AI technology through high-caliber, domain-informed contributions. In this role, you'll apply your expertise to help train next-generation AI systems. Your work will shape how models learn, reason, and perform through high-quality, real-world input. No prior experience in AI is required — your domain knowledge is what matters. This contract is ideal for specialists who routinely develop or review research papers, analytical reports, technical documentation, experiment summaries, or data-driven recommendations. An analytical mindset, meticulous attention to detail, and excellent written communication are essential as you craft, evaluate, and refine prompts, assess AI outputs, and ensure robust data quality. Perform independent research to validate facts, enhance data quality, and support robust content evaluation processes. Contribute to quality assurance efforts by annotating data, fact-checking outputs, and ensuring adherence to analytic standards. Interpret and summarize complex datasets or findings into clear, actionable reports and technical summaries. Collaborate asynchronously with project leads and other domain experts to share insights and best practices.  Preferred Qualifications  3+ years of professional experience in Data Science, Machine Learning, Applied AI, Statistics, Quantitative Analytics, or Data Analytics. Proven track record of producing or reviewing research papers, analytical reports, experiment summaries, notebooks, or technical documentation. Exceptional analytical reasoning, critical thinking, and attention to detail in written and quantitative deliverables. Advanced proficiency in professional writing, report writing, and business or technical communication. Experience with data annotation, content review, or rubric-based evaluation is highly desirable. Background in prompt engineering, AI output evaluation, fact checking, or RLHF (Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback) is advantageous but not required. Master's, JD, MBA, PhD, or other advanced degree preferred; contributors from highly selective universities, leading technology companies, or research organizations are strongly encouraged to express interest. Role Title: Workflow Annotator — Finance & Data Analysis  Role Type: Contractor  Location: Remote  micro1 is engaging Workflow Annotator — Finance & Data Analysis experts to contribute to a customer project focused on enhancing real-world finance and data workflows. In this role, you'll apply your expertise to help train next-generation AI systems. Your work will shape how models learn, reason, and perform through high-quality, real-world input. No prior experience in AI is required — your domain knowledge is what matters. This engagement centers on capturing, annotating, and documenting authentic, multi-step finance workflows using data warehouse and spreadsheet tools, with a strong emphasis on accuracy, realism, and security. Assignments are carried out in a contract/freelance capacity via Agora, our internal annotation platform, with flexible, self-directed participation and clear project deliverables.  Scope of Work  Design and document detailed, multi-step finance/data analyst workflows reflecting authentic tasks such as data pulls, reconciliation, model building, reporting, and analysis. Independently execute each workflow using real tools (e.g., Snowflake, Excel/Sheets), capturing every step, decision, and relevant input/output. Integrate adjacent collaboration tools naturally into workflows, such as Slack threads for cross-functional coordination or shared documents for distribution and feedback. Provide comprehensive documentation for each scenario in Agora, including clear step-by-step instructions, rationale, formulas, queries, and illustrative screenshots or results. Identify and flag ambiguous or edge-case scenarios to the project team and incorporate feedback to improve workflow accuracy and coverage. Uphold high standards of data security and privacy in all documentation and workflow execution.  Preferred Qualifications  Minimum 2 years of hands-on experience in FP&A, accounting, financial analysis, or data analytics with practical exposure to advanced spreadsheets and data warehousing tools (e.g., Snowflake). Ability to write robust SQL queries for reporting, reconciliation, and ad hoc analysis tasks. Demonstrated accuracy, attention to detail, and reliability in producing financial workflows and documentation. Strong written and verbal communication skills, with the ability to explain complex steps and context to annotators unfamiliar with the work. Familiarity with collaborative tools such as Slack and Microsoft 365/Google Workspace as part of finance/data workflows. Experience with financial modeling, forecasting, or BI dashboards (Looker, Tableau, Power BI) is a plus. Background in data annotation, process documentation, or QA is an advantage. Interested?   > Visual Discrimination Process Adherence Written Clarity Attention to Detail About micro1 micro1 is the leading AI data lab for training frontier models and evaluating AI agents. Experts contribute their diverse subject matter knowledge across domains such as finance, healthcare, STEM engineering, and more. micro1 transforms that real-world expertise into high-quality training data, evaluations, and feedback loops that improve how AI systems learn, reason, and perform. Role Title: Data Labeling Associate - Video Content  Role Type: Contractor  Location: Remote, worldwide  micro1 is engaging Data Labeling Associate - Video Content experts to contribute to a high-impact project for a valued customer. In this role, you'll apply your expertise to help train next-generation AI systems. Your work will shape how models learn, reason, and perform through high-quality, real-world input. No prior experience in AI is required - your domain knowledge is what matters.  Scope of Work  Review and annotate short first-person POV videos depicting household tasks, focusing on precise and consistent labeling. Identify and mark hamper position as left or right of the person for each video segment. Determine and tag the exact start and end frames for each pick-and-place movement within the video. Assign and record which of the seven numbered piles each clothing item is placed into. Adhere strictly to detailed annotation guidelines provided during onboarding and throughout the engagement. Maintain high quality and consistency across multiple videos within each session, ensuring accurate, actionable data for AI training. Flag ambiguous, unclear, or unusual cases for further review in accordance with project standards.  Preferred Qualifications  Exceptional visual discrimination skills and a keen eye for subtle details and frame-level changes. Proven ability to follow complex instructions with precision and incorporate feedback promptly. Experience with web-based video tools, such as playback controls, frame stepping, and timestamp-based interfaces. Strong focus and consistency in performing repetitive, detail-oriented tasks across multiple assignments. Reliable high-speed internet connection and a computer suitable for smooth video playback.  Nice to Have (but not required)  Prior experience in video annotation, data labeling, transcription, content moderation, or QA work Familiarity with any annotation platform (CVAT, Labelbox, Scale, V7, Encord, or similar) Background in roles requiring sustained attention to repetitive detail.  Start Timeline & Availability  We typically fill roles within 48 hours, so we're looking for teammates ready to jump in. If selected, we'd love for you to start your first task as soon as you move forward with your application. The expectation is to begin within ~24 hours of completing onboarding.  Interested? Experts contribute their diverse subject matter knowledge across domains such as finance, healthcare, STEM engineering, and more. micro1 transforms that real-world expertise into high-quality training data, evaluations, and feedback loops that improve how AI systems learn, reason, and perform. We aim to enable 1 billion people to do meaningful work by applying their expertise to AI. As our global expert network grows, micro1 is building the human intelligence layer for frontier AI.  micro1 is looking for small business owners who want to participate in a paid video data collection project for AI.  Your business would help collect real videos of normal work happening in your workplace. These videos will help train AI systems to better understand how people complete real-world tasks.  You do not need experience with AI. We are looking for business owners with active teams and real day-to-day work.  Who can participate  Your business may be a good fit if you: Are an owner, partner, or co-owner of a business in LATAM, the United States, or Canada Have at least 2 employees who can participate Have regular work activities that can be recorded safely Can have each participating employee record at least 10 hours of work Can get permission to record in the workplace Can make sure participating employees agree to be recorded Can start soon after a short onboarding call Employees will use their own iPhone or Android phone to record. Their phone must have an ultra-wide camera and the required motion sensors.  Businesses that may be a good fit  Examples include:  Construction, HVAC, plumbing, electrical, painting, and renovation businesses Auto repair, equipment repair, and maintenance businesses Machine shops, fabrication shops, woodworking, and manufacturing businesses Warehouses, logistics, delivery, and fulfillment businesses Landscaping, cleaning, pest control, and property maintenance businesses Catering companies, bakeries, restaurants, and commercial kitchens Hardware stores, garden centers, greenhouses, and nurseries The best fit is a business where employees do different hands-on tasks throughout the day. Assembly-line or highly repetitive work is not a fit.  Privacy and consent  We take privacy seriously. Before recording starts, the business owner must make sure employees agree to participate and that the business has permission to record.  Payment  Businesses are paid based on the amount and quality of approved video data collected.  Requirements  You own or help run a business in LATAM, the United States, or Canada You have at least 2 employees who can participate Each employee can record at least 10 hours. Interested? Role Title: Software Engineer (Go, Python, TS)  Role Type: Contractor  Location: Remote  micro1 is engaging Software Engineer (Go, Python, TS) experts to contribute to a pioneering customer project. In this role, you'll apply your expertise to help train next-generation AI systems. Your work will shape how models learn, reason, and perform through high-quality, real-world input. No prior experience in AI is required — your domain knowledge is what matters. This opportunity is ideal for contributors with a strong background in Go as their primary language, and solid proficiency in Python and TypeScript as secondary skills.  Scope of Work  Design and implement code solutions using Go, Python, and TypeScript to simulate real-world development scenarios for AI model training. Review, analyze, and critique sample code and technical materials, providing detailed written feedback that enhances AI understanding of software engineering concepts. Generate high-quality code samples, unit tests, and documentation to support a wide range of AI training use cases. Identify edge cases, best practices, and common pitfalls in software design and development to help refine model outputs. Contribute to discussions and collaborative reviews that focus on effective communication of complex technical ideas. Refine prompts and queries that will be used to evaluate and improve AI-generated code outputs.  Preferred Qualifications  Expert-level proficiency in Go, with substantial hands-on project experience. Strong command of Python and TypeScript, with the ability to write clean, idiomatic code in both languages. Excellent written and verbal communication skills, with a keen focus on articulating technical concepts clearly. Familiarity with software architecture, design patterns, and modern development workflows. Prior experience reviewing or analyzing code for correctness, efficiency, and clarity. Exposure to code documentation best practices and collaborative software projects. Interest in contributing to AI and machine learning advancements; direct AI experience is a plus but not required. Role Title: Physics Expert (AMO / Quantum Information)  Role Type: Contractor  Location: Remote  micro1 is engaging Physics Experts (AMO / Quantum Information) to contribute to a cutting-edge research-focused project in collaboration with a customer. In this role, you'll apply your expertise to help train next-generation AI systems. Your work will shape how models learn, reason, and perform through high-quality, real-world input. No prior experience in AI is required — your domain knowledge is what matters.  This opportunity centers on benchmarking advanced concepts in quantum optics, focusing on cascaded optical parametric amplifiers, SU(1,1) interferometers, and the effects of loss and two-mode squeezing. Contributors may participate as Solvers, Auditors, or Adjudicators, with assignments tailored to subfield specialization, experience, and demonstrated hands-on ability using the specified methods.  Scope of Work  Analyze and model cascaded optical parametric amplifiers and SU(1,1) interferometric systems under realistic loss mechanisms. Apply Bogoliubov transformations and covariance-matrix techniques to quantify quantum correlations and noise properties. Derive and interpret sideband photocurrent spectra utilizing homodyne detection methodologies. Implement and critique loss modeling via fictitious beamsplitters, ensuring robust benchmarking against theoretical and experimental scenarios. Utilize squeeze-parameter hyperbolic identities to characterize entanglement and squeezing performance in lossy quantum systems.  Preferred Qualifications  Advanced degree (PhD or equivalent experience) in Atomic, Molecular, and Optical (AMO) Physics, Quantum Optics, or Quantum Information Science. Proven expertise with Bogoliubov transformations, covariance-matrix formalism, and quantum noise analysis. Hands-on familiarity with sideband spectra, homodyne detection, and modeling of quantum optical loss mechanisms. Demonstrated ability applying squeeze-parameter hyperbolic identities in practical research or experimental analysis. Familiarity with frontier research involving cascaded parametric amplifiers, SU(1,1) interferometry, or two-mode squeezing is highly valued. Interested? Scope of Work  Analyze and provide expert insights into complex statistical physics phenomena, with a focus on replicated random-bond Ising/Ashkin-Teller models, the toric-code threshold, and the Nishimori line. Scope of Work  Provide expert insights on atomic, molecular, and optical (AMO) physics, with an emphasis on the optical properties of materials. Review, analyze, and discuss ab initio configuration interaction methods for calculating polarizability in atomic systems. Offer guidance and critique on atomic-structure calculations, specifically for Yb-171 and Yb-174 isotopes. Identify and differentiate between sigma and pi transitions in relevant atomic spectra, explaining underlying physical principles. Participate in adaptive, spoken AI interviews, responding to one question at a time and providing clear, concrete examples from legally shareable research or projects.  Preferred Qualifications  Advanced degree (Ms,PhD or equivalent experience) in Physics, with a focus on AMO physics or optical properties of materials. Proven experience with ab initio quantum chemistry calculations, specifically configuration interaction approaches to polarizability. Deep understanding of atomic-structure computation techniques, particularly as applied to ytterbium isotopes (Yb-171 and Yb-174). Strong capability to identify and explain sigma vs pi transitions in atomic contexts. Demonstrated aptitude for clear, structured written and verbal scientific communication.  Interested? Scope of Work  Conduct qualitative and quantitative chemical analyses and experiments to support product development, quality control, or process optimization. Strong written and verbal communication skills, with the ability to convey technical concepts clearly. Experience contributing to interdisciplinary projects or cross-functional collaborations. Familiarity with quality control protocols, safety standards, and regulatory guidelines is a plus. Interested? Role Title: Medical Writer / Clinical Document Author  Role Type: Contractor  Location: Remote  micro1 is engaging Medical Writers / Clinical Document Authors to participate in a customer’s project focused on developing advanced AI-assisted writing tools for clinical documentation. In this role, you'll apply your expertise to help train next-generation AI systems. Your work will shape how models learn, reason, and perform through high-quality, real-world input. No prior experience in AI is required — your domain knowledge is what matters.  Scope of Work  Author and review realistic evaluation tasks based on Clinical Study Reports, DSURs, PSURs/PBRERs, and related clinical documents to inform AI tool development. Apply expert judgment to assess whether AI-generated content fulfills clinical template and structural requirements, including ICH E3 organization, section sequencing, cross-referencing, and appendix management. Evaluate scientific accuracy in narrative sections such as efficacy, safety summaries, discussion, and conclusions, distinguishing true scientific or interpretive errors from stylistic differences. Trace narrative claims to source records—tables, figures, listings, and protocols—to verify that all assertions are appropriately supported. Provide clear and structured written rationales for each assessment, enabling precise diagnosis and improvement of AI model behavior. Collaborate with project leads to refine evaluation frameworks and document best practices for clinical regulatory writing in the context of AI.  Preferred Qualifications  Minimum 5 years of regulatory medical writing experience at a sponsor, CRO, or as an independent consultant. Direct experience independently authoring or leading the authoring of full Clinical Study Reports, beyond summaries or partial contributions. Fluency with ICH E3 and conventions for periodic safety documentation (ICH E2F, ICH E2C) and eCTD placement. Demonstrated ability to read and interpret TFLs and protocol documents, identifying where narrative diverges from source data. Exceptional written and verbal communication skills, especially in providing structured, actionable feedback on clinical content. Advanced degree in life sciences, pharmacy, or medicine (PhD, PharmD, MD, MSc), or equivalent depth of authoring experience. Experience across multiple clinical phases and therapeutic areas; oncology and haematology expertise is especially valued.  Join our customer's team and leverage your authoring expertise to help design the next generation of AI-assisted clinical documentation solutions. This high-bar role offers you the chance to shape how tomorrow's writing teams draft and review regulatory documents — through your expert input, judgment, and real-world experience.  Interested? Role Title: Computational Biology & Cheminformatics Expert  Role Type: Contractor  Location: Remote  micro1 is engaging Computational Biology & Cheminformatics Experts to contribute their expertise to a customer’s computational drug discovery project. In this role, you'll apply your expertise to help train next-generation AI systems. Your work will shape how models learn, reason, and perform through high-quality, real-world input. No prior experience in AI is required — your domain knowledge is what matters.  Scope of Work  Analyze and interpret small-molecule and drug discovery datasets using advanced computational biology, bioinformatics, and cheminformatics methods. Curate, annotate, and validate chemical and biological datasets (e.g., ChEMBL, PubChem, DrugBank) to support AI-driven discovery platforms. Evaluate compound-target interactions, ADMET properties, and lead optimization strategies by integrating chemical, biological, and clinical data sources. Provide expert insights on structure-activity and structure-property relationships (SAR/SPR), medicinal chemistry approaches, and experimental design considerations. Build and implement code-based benchmark tasks (e.g., terminal/CLI-based environments) that reflect realistic computational drug discovery scenarios. Develop reproducible environments (e.g., using Docker) and automated testing pipelines to ensure task correctness and solvability. Assess and review AI-generated outputs for scientific rigor, accuracy, and practical relevance, delivering detailed written feedback and recommendations.  Preferred Qualifications  Advanced expertise in Computational Biology, Cheminformatics, Medicinal Chemistry, Biochemistry, or related fields; advanced degree (PhD, MSc, PharmD) highly valued but not strictly required. Strong coding proficiency in Python (beyond analysis scripts), with hands-on experience building tools, pipelines, or testable code; familiarity with Git, GitHub, and Docker. Extensive experience with cheminformatics toolkits and platforms such as RDKit, KNIME, Schrödinger, OpenEye, or MOE. Proven track record in small-molecule drug discovery, SAR/QSAR evaluation, ADMET prediction, or virtual screening workflows. Comfort working with public chemical and bioactivity databases and integrating diverse datasets for scientific analysis. Demonstrated ability to clearly communicate complex chemical and biological concepts in written feedback and reports. Experience participating in multidisciplinary and/or remote projects; familiarity with AI-assisted coding tools is a plus. Interested? We aim to enable 1 billion people to do meaningful work by applying their expertise to AI. As our global expert network grows, micro1 is building the human intelligence layer for frontier AI.  Job Title: Uzbek Bilingual Expert  Job Type: Part time  Location: Remote  Job Summary:  Join our customer's team as a Uzbek Bilingual Expert and play a pivotal role in elevating the quality and accuracy of language tasks. In this dynamic position, you will leverage your fluency in Uzbek and English to perform in-depth video transcriptions, analyze speaker emotion, and refine complex linguistic nuances—all from the comfort of your home.  Key Responsibilities:  Transcribe Uzbek-language video content with meticulous attention to accuracy and clarity. Clearly outline and annotate precise timestamps for dialogue and scene changes. Analyze and describe the emotional tone and intent of speakers, capturing subtleties and mood shifts. Conduct advanced grammar, syntax, and tone analysis to ensure linguistic fidelity. Define and explain the conceptual meaning of words and idiomatic expressions within context. Collaborate with the customer's team to deliver insightful linguistic feedback and suggestions for improvement. Consistently meet complex language task requirements, adhering to a minimum commitment of 15 hours per week.  Required Skills and Qualifications:  Native or near-native proficiency in both Uzbek and English languages. Exceptional written and verbal communication skills, with a keen eye for linguistic detail. Proven experience in transcription, translation, or language analysis. Strong understanding of Uzbek and English grammar, syntax, and cultural context. Ability to identify, articulate, and describe speaker emotion and content tone. Technological proficiency and comfort working with digital content and collaborative platforms. High level of self-motivation and commitment to delivering quality work independently.  Preferred Qualifications:  Degree in Linguistics, Philology, Translation Studies, or related field. Experience with linguistic annotation or corpus analysis tools. Background in educational, research, or media translation projects. Interested? Role Title: Autodesk Inventor Specialist  Role Type: Contractor  Location: Remote  micro1 is engaging Autodesk Inventor Specialists to contribute to a customer-focused project involving advanced mechanical design. In this role, you'll apply your expertise to help train next-generation AI systems. Your work will shape how models learn, reason, and perform through high-quality, real-world input. No prior experience in AI is required — your domain knowledge is what matters.  Scope of Work  Develop fully-parametric 3D CAD models of mechanical parts and assemblies based on detailed written briefs using Autodesk Inventor (or comparable platforms). Edit and update existing models to reflect revised requirements, ensuring all changes remain fully parametric and in line with best practices. Audit and review CAD models for compliance with dimensional, tolerance, and manufacturability criteria, identifying and documenting any gaps or issues. Create and interpret detailed technical drawings, applying ASME Y14.5 or equivalent GD&T standards. Ensure robust Design for Manufacturability (DFM) considerations across processes such as injection molding, sheet metal, CNC machining, and casting/forging. Support AI training initiatives by providing clear documentation and rationale for design decisions, edits, and audits. Communicate effectively in written and verbal formats, delivering precise feedback and solution-oriented insights to project stakeholders.  Preferred Qualifications  Minimum 4 years of hands-on parametric modeling experience for edit-level tasks; 10+ years for complex creation work. Expertise in Autodesk Inventor, with fluency in similar platforms like FreeCAD, SolidWorks, or Fusion 360 considered highly relevant. Demonstrated proficiency with GD&T (ASME Y14.5 or equivalent), with practical, real-world application. Proven DFM experience covering at least two to three manufacturing processes (e.g., injection molding, sheet metal, CNC, casting). Experience with reverse engineering workflows, such as scan-to-CAD or point cloud-to-model reconstruction. Ability to manage assembly-level designs and complex mechanical systems, not just single-part modeling. Familiarity with CAD automation, scripting (Python, iLogic, VBA, NX Open), or similar tools for enhancing workflow efficiency. Interested? We aim to enable 1 billion people to do meaningful work by applying their expertise to AI. As our global expert network grows, micro1 is building the human intelligence layer for frontier AI.  Role Title: Clinician (Oncology / Hematology)  Role Type: Contractor  Location: Remote  micro1 is engaging Clinician (Oncology / Hematology)s to contribute to a customer’s project at the forefront of AI in healthcare. In this role, you'll apply your expertise to help train next-generation AI systems. Your work will shape how models learn, reason, and perform through high-quality, real-world input. No prior experience in AI is required — your domain knowledge is what matters.  Scope of Work  Author and review evaluation tasks that require nuanced clinical judgment, leveraging Clinical Study Reports, periodic safety reports, and regulatory correspondence in oncology and haematology. Assess the clinical defensibility of efficacy and safety conclusions, ensuring endpoints and effect sizes reflect true clinical significance. Apply advanced judgment to dose escalation, dose-limiting toxicity, modifications, adverse event grading and attribution, and the coherence of patient narratives. Evaluate benefit-risk reasoning, scrutinizing the durability of conclusions in light of new events, and the realism of described management strategies. Identify and flag content that is clinically implausible or internally inconsistent, distinguishing these from legitimate clinical variation. Provide structured, guideline-based written rationales that clearly explain the validity or limitations of clinical interpretations for a non-clinical audience.  Preferred Qualifications  Specialist registration or board certification in medical oncology and/or haematology, with 5+ years of post-training clinical experience. Direct involvement in clinical trials as investigator, sub-investigator, or sponsor-side medical monitor, safety physician, or clinical development physician. Proficiency with CTCAE grading, relevant response criteria (RECIST, Lugano, IMWG, IWCLL, ELN), and outcome endpoints (ORR, DoR, PFS, OS, MRD). Hands-on experience with trial documents such as protocols, investigator brochures, CSRs, safety narratives, or DSMB/IDMC materials. Demonstrated excellence in written communication, with clear, structured explanations of clinical reasoning tailored for non-clinical readers. Previous roles in medical monitoring or as medical director on oncology or haematology programmes, and committee participation (IDMC, DSMB, adjudication, or independent review committees). Peer-reviewed publication record or authorship on clinical trial manuscripts, with any exposure to AI-assisted clinical review tools seen as a plus. Interested? Role Title: Autodesk Fusion 360 Specialist  Role Type: Contractor  Location: Remote  micro1 is engaging Autodesk Fusion 360 Specialists to contribute expertise on a high-impact customer project focused on advanced parametric CAD modeling. In this role, you'll apply your expertise to help train next-generation AI systems. Your work will shape how models learn, reason, and perform through high-quality, real-world input. No prior experience in AI is required — your domain knowledge is what matters.  Scope of Work  Develop fully parametric 3D models of mechanical parts and assemblies based on detailed written briefs, emphasizing robust sketching, constraints, and feature trees. Edit and update existing parametric CAD models to reflect evolving requirements, ensuring manufacturability and compliance with specifications. Audit and review CAD models for dimensional accuracy, tolerance application, and alignment with design intent, calling out any gaps or deviations. Support AI model training by delivering consistent, high-quality CAD data and clear documentation of design decisions. Apply deep working knowledge of manufacturing processes—such as injection molding, sheet metal fabrication, CNC machining, and casting/forging—to optimize designs for production. Communicate design rationale, feedback, and technical insights with clarity—both in writing and verbally—in support of collaboration and continuous project improvement.  Preferred Qualifications  Extensive hands-on experience with parametric CAD platforms (ideally FreeCAD, with strong proficiency in Autodesk Fusion 360, SolidWorks, or Inventor acceptable). Proven application of GD&T principles (ASME Y14.5 or equivalent) in real-world projects. Demonstrated DFM expertise across at least 2-3 manufacturing processes relevant to the project scope. 4+ years of applied experience for model editing tasks; 10+ years for complex model creation assignments. Proven ability to tackle assembly-level modeling, not just single-part tasks. Experience in reverse engineering workflows, such as scan-to-CAD or point cloud conversions, is highly valued. Familiarity with CAD automation or scripting (e.g., Python, iLogic, NX Open, VBA) considered a strong plus for automating and standardizing workflows. Interested? Role Title: Pharmacovigilance Expert  Role Type: Contractor  Location: Remote  micro1 is engaging Pharmacovigilance Experts to contribute their advanced drug safety expertise on a key customer project. In this role, you'll apply your expertise to help train next-generation AI systems. Your work will shape how models learn, reason, and perform through high-quality, real-world input. No prior experience in AI is required — your domain knowledge is what matters. This opportunity is ideal for professionals experienced in authoring and reviewing complex safety reports, passionate about data quality, and committed to clear written and verbal communication.  Scope of Work  Author and review evaluation tasks based on DSURs, PSURs/PBRERs, and associated safety data and case-level records. Apply expert judgment to assess the accuracy and adequacy of interval safety findings, signal evaluations, and benefit-risk conclusions in alignment with best practices. Assess template and structural conformity to ICH E2F and ICH E2C requirements, including proper section content, presentation of cumulative versus interval data, and completeness of appendices. Reconcile figures and case counts across report sections and data sources, identifying and documenting discrepancies significant for regulatory review. Evaluate safety conclusions in light of new data or events, and provide clear, structured written rationales supporting your determinations. Deliver structured feedback to inform the development of AI-driven tools for pharmacovigilance documentation and analysis.  Preferred Qualifications  5+ years of pharmacovigilance or drug safety experience at a sponsor, CRO, or as an independent consultant. Demonstrated proficiency in authoring or reviewing DSURs and/or PSURs/PBRERs from start to finish. Working fluency with ICH E2F, ICH E2C(R2), and GVP guidelines, including signal management and benefit-risk assessment methodology. Hands-on experience with MedDRA coding, seriousness and causality assessment, expectedness determination, and aggregate safety data analysis. Expertise in reconciling aggregate safety data and identifying inconsistencies with case-level information. Advanced degree in life sciences, pharmacy, nursing, or medicine (PharmD, MD, MSc, or equivalent). Experience in both development-stage and post-marketing safety reporting, QPPV or deputy QPPV roles, safety committee participation, or AI-assisted safety review tools is a plus. Interested? Role Title: Biostatistician  Role Type: Contractor  Location: Remote  micro1 is engaging Biostatisticians to contribute their clinical statistics expertise to a dynamic customer project focused on AI-assisted clinical research. In this role, you'll apply your expertise to help train next-generation AI systems. Your work will shape how models learn, reason, and perform through high-quality, real-world input. No prior experience in AI is required — your domain knowledge is what matters.  Scope of Work  Author and review evaluation tasks that require deriving, reproducing, or validating statistical outputs from clinical datasets and associated tables, figures, and listings (TFLs). Apply expert judgment to assess the correctness and consistency of reported estimates, confidence intervals, p-values, analysis populations, and missing data handling in alignment with the statistical analysis plan (SAP). Identify discrepancies between statistical outputs and their narrative descriptions in clinical study reports (CSR), including subtle errors in population definitions, censoring rules, or multiplicity handling. Establish defensible ground truth for each evaluation task, documenting the derivation process to enable independent verification. Provide structured written rationales distinguishing true statistical errors from acceptable methodological alternatives, employing clear and concise communication. Collaborate with a multidisciplinary project team, providing statistical insights and feedback as needed to refine evaluation tasks and criteria.  Preferred Qualifications  5+ years as a biostatistician supporting clinical trials at a sponsor, CRO, or academic trials unit. Hands-on experience producing or quality controlling TFLs for regulatory submissions and working directly from CDISC SDTM/ADaM datasets. Solid understanding of statistical methods used in confirmatory trials, including survival analysis, mixed models, covariate adjustment, multiplicity control, and estimand and missing-data strategies under ICH E9(R1). Proficiency in SAS and/or R, with the ability to independently reproduce analyses from written specifications. Ability to interpret SAPs and ensure reported results are consistent with pre-specified analyses. Advanced degree (MSc or PhD) in Biostatistics, Statistics, or a closely related quantitative field. Nice to have: Experience as lead statistician on pivotal/registrational studies, authoring or reviewing CSR statistical sections, oncology endpoint expertise, and exposure to AI-assisted statistical review tools. Interested? Exceptional written and verbal communication skills, with a focus on articulating technical concepts clearly. Prior experience contributing to technical publications, patents, or collaborative R&D projects is a plus.  Compensation Structure  Compensation is output-based; experts are paid per task that meets the project specifications.   > Required skills: live music performance, musical instrument proficiency, public performance, 7+, $100-300/h.  live music performance musical instrument proficiency public performance event logistics coordination self-promotion (e.g., sharing portfolio or social media links) time management permit and compliance awareness audio/video recording compliance professionalism adaptability About micro1 micro1 is the leading AI data lab for training frontier models and evaluating AI agents. Experts contribute their diverse subject matter knowledge across domains such as finance, healthcare, STEM engineering, and more. micro1 transforms that real-world expertise into high-quality training data, evaluations, and feedback loops that improve how AI systems learn, reason, and perform.  Our platform identifies and vets top talent through an AI recruiter, enabling high-quality expert contributions at scale. We aim to enable 1 billion people to do meaningful work by applying their expertise to AI. As our global expert network grows, micro1 is building the human intelligence layer for frontier AI.  About Us  At micro1, we work with human experts across a wide range of fields to build more reliable AI systems. Human expertise is at the center of everything we do, and we believe the best technology creates more opportunities for people to create, perform, and discover.  In the first edition of microsessions, we're inviting local musicians to perform live in outdoor areas across San Francisco on August 6–7. We'll sponsor artists to play in some of the city's most iconic public spaces, introduce their music to new audiences, and professionally capture their performances as part of a project exploring the future of music education.  We are creating this as something musicians enjoy being part of, and something San Francisco enjoys stumbling upon.  What You'll Be Doing  We're looking for 20–40 solo musicians and small groups (1–3 performers) to perform live across San Francisco on Thursday, August 6 and Friday, August 7.  Selected musicians will:  Perform a 1–2 hour live set during one of three daily time slots: LUNCH - 11:00 AM – 1:00 PM AFTERNOON - 2:00 PM – 4:00 PM HAPPY HOUR - 5:00 PM – 8:00 PM Perform at one of several high-foot-traffic locations across the city, including FiDi, the Marina, Dolores Park, Alamo Square, Pier 39, and Golden Gate Park (locations will be finalized with each musician before the event but musicians must ultimately secure their desired locations and any associated logistics) Wear micro1 event apparel during the performance + feature basic signage explaining microsessions. Be filmed and recorded by a small production crew for approximately 20-60 minutes during their set. About the Recording - What It's For (and What It's Not)  Every performance will be filmed and recorded. We're upfront about this on-site and in this brief because we think a project about honoring musicians should be honest with everyone it touches. Here's exactly what the recording is used for:  Marketing: a launch film and social content celebrating the artists and the city. Education-focused research: audio can help AI models better understand music — things like recognizing instruments and genres, learning music theory (keys, chord progressions), and understanding the cultural roots of a style, as well as potentially enhancing artist discoverability. The goal is tools that help people learn and discover music, not replace musicians or their work.  What we will never do:  Use this data to generate artificial music, ever. We don’t allow it and we don’t appreciate it. We are making a public promise to reserve this data for tools that support musicians, never extract from them. Use it to replace or compete with musicians. Use it for anything beyond the education and marketing purposes described above.  Musicians are compensated and every performer participates with full consent and a signed release covering the licensing described here.  Who We're Looking For Solo musicians or groups of 1–3 people. Available for a 1–2 hour set within one of our time slots between August 6–7 (playing on both days is welcome if you're interested). Able to perform at a location you're already permitted or approved to play at (whether by city permit or private-property arrangement) — final locations are subject to our approval but we are not able to coordinate permits at this time. Responsible for your own instruments/equipment; please build any related costs into your quoted rate. Able to share a video, Instagram, or portfolio link showing a past performance. Comfortable being filmed and recorded per the terms above. Interested?   > Required skills: Captura de Video, Atención al Detalle, Tareas Domésticas, $10-15/h.  Captura de Video Atención al Detalle Tareas Domésticas About micro1 micro1 is the leading AI data lab for training frontier models and evaluating AI agents. Experts contribute their diverse subject matter knowledge across domains such as finance, healthcare, STEM engineering, and more. micro1 transforms that real-world expertise into high-quality training data, evaluations, and feedback loops that improve how AI systems learn, reason, and perform. Título del Puesto: Graba Videos desde Casa (Estados Unidos)  Tipo de Contratación: Contratista (Contractor)  Ubicación: Remoto – Candidatos ubicados en los siguientes estados:  Alabama, Alaska, Arkansas, Delaware, Florida, Georgia, Idaho, Indiana, Iowa, Kansas, Kentucky, Louisiana, Maryland, Massachusetts, Michigan, Minnesota, Mississippi, Missouri, Montana, Nebraska, Nevada, New Hampshire, Nuevo México, Carolina del Norte, Dakota del Norte, Ohio, Oklahoma, Pensilvania, Carolina del Sur, Dakota del Sur, Tennessee, Utah, Vermont, Virginia, Virginia Occidental, Wisconsin y Wyoming.  Resumen del Puesto  En este rol, utilizarás tus habilidades para contribuir al entrenamiento de sistemas de Inteligencia Artificial de última generación. Tu trabajo ayudará directamente a que estos modelos aprendan, razonen y ejecuten tareas mediante datos reales y de alta calidad.  No es necesario tener experiencia previa en Inteligencia Artificial. Lo más importante es tu capacidad para seguir instrucciones y generar datos de alta calidad.  Responsabilidades Principales  Capturar datos precisos de movimiento utilizando los sensores IMU de tu smartphone mientras realizas actividades físicas específicas. Grabar videos sincronizados para garantizar la precisión de los datos y la integridad de toda la información de movimiento recopilada. Seguir protocolos técnicos detallados para cumplir de manera consistente con los estándares de calidad, etiquetado y estandarización del proyecto. Entregar un mínimo de 10 horas semanales de videos aprobados y de alta calidad durante todo el proyecto. Comunicarte de forma clara y proactiva con el equipo para resolver dudas y compartir observaciones sobre el proceso de recolección de datos. Cumplir de manera constante con los plazos y entregables establecidos por el proyecto. Participar en una verificación de compatibilidad del dispositivo y en un proceso de entrevista asistido por IA durante el onboarding.  Requisitos  Contar con una banda para sujetar el celular en la cabeza (head strap), capaz de capturar ambas manos en el video, dentro de las 48 horas posteriores a la fecha de inicio. Tener un smartphone compatible. Los dispositivos compatibles incluyen: iPhone 12 o superior. Google Pixel 6 o superior. Samsung Galaxy S21 o superior. Excelente atención al detalle y capacidad demostrada para seguir procedimientos estandarizados. Capacidad para gestionar entregas de forma consistente y cumplir plazos en un entorno dinámico y orientado a datos. Excelentes habilidades de comunicación escrita y verbal para documentar información y colaborar de forma remota. Facilidad para utilizar tecnología y experiencia con dispositivos móviles para la captura de video y datos de sensores. Condición física para realizar tareas repetitivas de movimiento de manera segura y eficiente. Acceso a un smartphone compatible capaz de recopilar datos con alta fidelidad.  Nota: Las postulaciones enviadas con una dirección de correo electrónico de Gmail serán consideradas un valor agregado, ya que facilitan la integración con las herramientas del proyecto.  Cualificaciones Deseables  Formación o experiencia en robótica, kinesiología, recolección de datos mediante sensores o áreas técnicas relacionadas. Experiencia en análisis del movimiento humano, segmentación de actividades o etiquetado de conjuntos de datos para aprendizaje automático (Machine Learning). Familiaridad con la organización y estructuración de grandes volúmenes de datos para aplicaciones de IA y robótica.  Estructura de Compensación  La compensación se basa en la producción. Los contratistas reciben un pago por cada video que cumpla con las especificaciones del proyecto.  El tiempo necesario para completar las tareas puede variar según la experiencia y el flujo de trabajo de cada participante.  Existen requisitos mínimos de entrega: los videos deben cumplir con la duración especificada por el proyecto y cada participante deberá enviar entre 10 y 15 horas de video por semana.  Inicio de Actividades y Disponibilidad  Normalmente completamos el proceso de selección en un plazo de 48 horas, por lo que buscamos personas con disponibilidad para comenzar rápidamente.  Si eres seleccionado(a), esperamos que inicies tu primera tarea tan pronto avances en el proceso de contratación.  Se espera que el inicio de las actividades ocurra aproximadamente 24 horas después de completar el onboarding.  Requisitos de Equipamiento  Smartphone  Todas las tareas del proyecto deberán realizarse utilizando un smartphone.  Los participantes grabarán su trabajo directamente desde el dispositivo móvil mientras realizan las actividades asignadas.  Banda para la Cabeza (Head Strap)  Las tareas requieren el uso de una cámara colocada en la cabeza mediante una banda para celular (head strap).  Los participantes grabarán videos en primera persona mientras realizan las actividades físicas.  Las especificaciones de la banda requerida y de cualquier equipo adicional se compartirán durante el proceso de onboarding. Interested? Título da Vaga: Gravador(a) de Vídeos em Casa (Estados Unidos)  Tipo de Contratação: Contractor  Localização: Remoto – Profissionais baseados nos seguintes estados: Alabama, Alaska, Arkansas, Delaware, Florida, Georgia, Idaho, Indiana, Iowa, Kansas, Kentucky, Louisiana, Maryland, Massachusetts, Michigan, Minnesota, Mississippi, Missouri, Montana, Nebraska, Nevada, New Hampshire, New Mexico, North Carolina, North Dakota, Ohio, Oklahoma, Pennsylvania, South Carolina, South Dakota, Tennessee, Utah, Vermont, Virginia, West Virginia, Wisconsin, and Wyoming.  Resumo da Vaga:  Nesta função, você utilizará sua experiência para ajudar no treinamento de sistemas de IA de última geração. Seu trabalho contribuirá diretamente para a forma como esses modelos aprendem, raciocinam e executam tarefas por meio de dados reais e de alta qualidade. Não é necessário ter experiência prévia com Inteligência Artificial — o mais importante é sua capacidade de seguir instruções e produzir dados de qualidade.  Principais Responsabilidades  Capturar dados precisos de movimento utilizando os sensores IMU do seu smartphone durante atividades físicas especificadas. Gravar vídeos sincronizados para garantir a precisão dos dados e a integridade de todas as informações de movimento coletadas. Seguir protocolos técnicos detalhados para atender consistentemente aos padrões de qualidade, rotulagem e padronização do projeto. Entregar no mínimo 10 horas semanais de vídeos aprovados e de alta qualidade durante todo o período do projeto. Comunicar-se de forma clara e proativa com a equipe para esclarecer dúvidas e compartilhar observações sobre o processo de coleta de dados. Garantir entregas pontuais e consistentes, em conformidade com os marcos e expectativas do projeto. Participar da verificação de compatibilidade do dispositivo e de um processo de entrevista inovador com suporte de IA durante o onboarding.  Requisitos  Ter acesso a uma faixa para fixação do celular na cabeça (head strap), capaz de registrar ambas as mãos, em até 48 horas após a data de início. Possuir um smartphone compatível. Dispositivos suportados incluem: iPhone 12 ou superior; Google Pixel 6 ou superior; Samsung Galaxy S21 ou superior. Excelente atenção aos detalhes e capacidade comprovada de seguir protocolos padronizados. Capacidade de gerenciar entregas de forma consistente e cumprir prazos em um ambiente dinâmico e orientado por dados. Excelentes habilidades de comunicação escrita e verbal para documentação e colaboração remota. Facilidade com tecnologia e experiência no uso de dispositivos móveis para captura de vídeo e dados de sensores. Condições físicas para realizar tarefas repetitivas de movimento com segurança e eficiência. Acesso a um smartphone compatível capaz de realizar coleta de dados com alta fidelidade.  Observação: Candidaturas enviadas utilizando um endereço de e-mail Gmail serão consideradas um diferencial, pois facilitam a integração com as ferramentas do projeto.  Qualificações Desejáveis  Formação ou experiência em robótica, cinesiologia, coleta de dados por sensores ou áreas técnicas relacionadas. Experiência com análise de movimento humano, segmentação de atividades ou rotulagem de conjuntos de dados para aprendizado de máquina. Familiaridade com organização e estruturação de grandes conjuntos de dados para aplicações de IA e robótica.  Estrutura de Remuneração  A remuneração é baseada na produção. Os profissionais são pagos por cada vídeo gravado que atenda às especificações do projeto. O tempo necessário para concluir as atividades pode variar de acordo com a experiência e o fluxo de trabalho de cada participante.  Há requisitos mínimos de entrega: os vídeos devem possuir a duração especificada pelo projeto, e cada profissional deverá enviar entre 10 e 15 horas de vídeo por semana.  Início das Atividades e Disponibilidade  Normalmente concluímos o processo de seleção em até 48 horas, portanto buscamos profissionais disponíveis para iniciar rapidamente. Caso seja selecionado(a), esperamos que você comece sua primeira tarefa assim que avançar no processo de candidatura. A expectativa é que o início das atividades ocorra aproximadamente 24 horas após a conclusão do onboarding .  Requisitos de Equipamentos  Smartphone  Todas as tarefas deste projeto deverão ser realizadas utilizando um smartphone. Os participantes gravarão seu fluxo de trabalho diretamente pelo dispositivo móvel enquanto executam as atividades.  Faixa para Cabeça (Head Strap)  As tarefas exigem o uso de uma câmera posicionada na cabeça por meio de uma faixa (head strap). Os participantes gravarão vídeos em primeira pessoa enquanto realizam as atividades físicas. As especificações da faixa necessária e de quaisquer equipamentos adicionais serão compartilhadas durante o processo de onboarding. Interested? ## 💡 Consejos Profesionales

1. **Adapta tu Perfil:** Asegúrate de que tu GitHub y LinkedIn destaquen las habilidades específicas solicitadas.
2. **Demuestra tu Talento:** Ten al menos un repositorio público sólido que demuestre tu experiencia.
3. **Actúa Rápido:** Estos roles de alta remuneración se cierran *extremadamente* rápido. Si ves que encajas, postúlate de inmediato.

---

## ⭐ Guardar y Apoyar

Si encuentras útil esta lista, por favor considera darle una **Estrella (⭐)** al repositorio en la parte superior derecha!

- 👀 **Observa (Watch)** el repositorio para recibir notificaciones de nuevos trabajos.
- 🔖 **Guárdalo (Bookmark)** en tu navegador y revisa diariamente.
- 📣 **Compártelo** con amigos que busquen roles remotos bien remunerados.
